HARNESS TEST CASES - in-game checks of BUGS_AND_QUIRKS entries (and other facts) in the real MoM 1.31, run on the
hidden desktop (tools/harness/). One block per case: what it checks, setup, steps, expected, result, how to re-run.
Saves are built from work/harness/SAVE9_orig.GAM with tools/harness/save_edit.py and written ONLY as the harness
copy's SAVE9.GAM; each script restores it afterwards. Combat tests are allowed (combat cases were added 2026-10-22): place the human and AI stacks side by side with
save_edit.py, then move into the battle. Results are copied to
work/reports/check_*.txt. Screen / control details: docs/ui/HARNESS_UI_NOTES.txt.

TC-203  numpad moves across the x = 0 seam                                   BUGS #203 -> GAME (2026-10-17)
  setup    all human units on patrol; Dragon Turtles 161 / 173 / 192 (swimmers, 2 moves) alone on Arcanus ocean at
           (0,20) / (0,25) / (0,30)
  steps    W until the turtle is selected; one keypad key each: kp4 (West), kp7 (North-West), kp1 (South-West)
  expected bug: (59,19), (59,23) via a 2-tile path, (59,30); correct: (59,20), (59,24), (59,31)
  result   (59,19) / (59,23) moves used up / (59,30) - the bug exactly; grey Left arrow = kp4
  control  --control: same keys at x = 2 -> (1,20), (1,24), (1,31) correct
  run      python -P tools/harness/check_203.py [--control]

TC-MAPGEN  does the generator keep continents off the seam? (owner question)       fact -> #203 / #27 / #131 reach
  setup    harness MAGIC.SET land size per run (0 / 1 / 2)
  steps    MAGIC.EXE: Esc, N, options OK, Merlin, Enter, Barbarian, green banner; read the world from RAM and keep the
           SAVE9.GAM MAGIC.EXE wrote
  result   30 runs (10 per size) + 9 older maps = 78 planes: no land on rows 2-37 in columns 58-1; nearest x = 2 / 57;
           seam land only on polar rows (0 / 39 all tundra, 1 / 38 tundra at the seam on most maps)
  run      python -P tools/harness/mapgen_survey.py N [small medium large]   -> work/reports/mapgen_survey.txt

TC-204  numpad move while viewing the other plane (no Planar Travel)          BUGS #204 -> GAME (2026-10-17)
  setup    all human units on patrol except hero 19 (ready, so the turn does not end by itself) and Halfling
           Bowmen 107 alone on Arcanus; three start tiles:
             A (25,24) West tile ocean on Arcanus, land on Myrror
             B (28,27) West tile land on Arcanus, water on Myrror
             C (27,29) West tile land on both
  steps    W until the Bowmen are selected; Plane button (290,9) -> view on Myrror; kp4
  expected old guess 'free plane shift'; alternative 'path planned on the viewed plane's map'
  result   no plane change ever; A: the Bowmen walk onto the Arcanus ocean; B: no move (unit set done); C: normal
  control  --control (no plane switch): A refused, B walks, C walks
  run      python -P tools/harness/check_204.py [--control]

TC-224  loading the autosave leaves the human's units 'done'                   BUGS #224 -> GAME (2026-10-17)
  setup    the game's own autosave from TC-204b (turn 108); copy with unit 68 set ready so the turn cannot end
  steps    load it (WIZARDS.EXE starts from slot 9); read every human unit from RAM at once
  expected bug: units that acted in turn 107 are stored finished / 0 moves and stay so after loading
  result   units 19, 105, 193: file and live = 0 moves, finished, status 4 - cannot act in turn 108 (in the
           uninterrupted game they had full moves). The unedited autosave (no unit ready): the game ended turn 108
           at once.
  run      python -P tools/harness/check_224.py [SAVE]

TC-173  Chaos Ward vs a Chaos creature on Go-To                               BUGS #173 -> GAME (2026-10-17)
  setup    all human units on patrol except hero 19 (ready) and unit 107 turned into Hell Hounds (type 155, Chaos)
           at (28,27), Go-To (status 3) to Dracon (29,27); Dracon gets Chaos Ward (City +0x4e = 1, own city)
  steps    D on the hero (the game runs the Go-Tos); then W, D, N; click the message box several times
  result   'A Chaos Ward prevents your troops from entering the city' reappears after every click (clicks are
           consumed), the Hounds keep their Go-To, nothing else can be done: stuck
  contrast --sorcery: Sorcery Ward + Phantom Beast (type 191): blocked once, Go-To cleared, game goes on
  control  --control: no ward, the Hounds walk in
  run      python -P tools/harness/check_173.py [--control | --sorcery]

  setup    tools/harness/mom_debug.conf (normal core, mcp_server=58991); dbgchan.py listens on 127.0.0.1:58991
  steps    start on the hidden desktop, PING, wait 25 s, BREAK, EV CS / IP / DS, BPLIST, LOGC 20000 (hex!), RUN

           LOGCPU.TXT in the DOSBox-X folder; RUN acknowledged

           starts with the Borland copyright

SAVE STATES: WORK with the nightly build (dosbox.EXE_NIGHTLY, upstream CI main-ai 433ac897): BREAK, then
  SAVESTATE / LOADSTATE <file>.sav over the channel (dbgchan.save_state / load_state). 0.35 s per save, about 0.9 MB.
  Baseline: work/harness/states/overland_start.sav (turn-0 map after start). Tested: load -> screen restored (only the
  animated node sparkle differs), DOSBox-X and the channel survive. 2026.10.01 (tools/dl/dosbox-x) still crashes on load.

TC-DIFF  What does one action write? (state-vs-state RAM diff)                  tool check (2026-10-17)
  setup    LOADSTATE overland_start.sav before every action; same 4 s wait with no action = idle diff (dropped)
  run      python -P tools/harness/unk_probe.py  -> work/harness/dbg/unk_probe.txt (memdiff labels: nearest name)
  then     python -P tools/harness/whowrites.py "KEYS" OFF..  (BPM on DGROUP:OFF; CS:IP after the write -> function)
  results  36aa:3060 / 305e outline flash (Outline_Cycle_Tick), 36aa:2f84 Music_Mood_Check counter, 3f33:0d3e magic
           screen background, 96D0 / 96F8 = map_unit_grid cells (Map_Build_Unit_Grid), BC74 = help_entries[25] (read
           from HLPENTRY.LBX: the write lands in the DOS call, F000:CA40), "scroll"/"hlpentry" upper-cased in place by
           LBX_Normalize_Name. Noise to ignore: DS:D59A.. near-heap scratch, DS:FC00.. stack.
